⚠ v5.2 retraction notice (2026-04-25): some DIA-AUC and DIAL numbers below were computed under v5.1 leaky-ComBat protocol, which fit ComBat on full pooled data before LODO split (information leak). Under proper per-fold ComBat, the numbers shift. Current submission-ready figures are at the v17 npj robustness page (manuscript v3, scenario-B reframe). See v5p2_critical_assessment.md for details.
